# marketplace digest 2026-11-15

> 本日报是**唯一完整的上架预告面**：下列 pass verdict 在本日报发布后进入成熟期（digest-appearance 门）。
> "no findings" ≠ safe.
> 📱 手机操作：点行的「审核单」链接开 issue → **Labels** 勾 `verdict:pass` 或 `verdict:concern` 即可写回裁决，零打字。
> 行标说明：⛔已被（包级/更新的）concern 刹车 = 该行已公告但不会放行；✅已被更新的 pass 解除 = concern 已被翻案；⚠️不在目录 = 该 verdict 针对的条目当前不在 index（已下架或未入库），公告照发但无槽可落。

| opencode-plugin-deepseek-peak | 1.2.0 | ai | 插件仅计算本地时间判断峰谷并渲染UI，无网络请求、凭据读取或存储，描述与实现一致 / The plugin only computes local time to display peak/off-peak state in the UI, makes no network calls, reads no credentials, and the description matches the code | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20opencode-plugin-deepseek-peak%22) |
| @glaicer/supercode-sandbox-awareness | 1.0.0 | ai | 该插件仅读取环境变量并注入只读提示，不执行网络调用、凭证获取或混淆，manifest描述与代码行为一致。 / The plugin only reads an env marker and injects read-only guidance with no network calls, credential handling, or obfuscation, and the manifest matches the code behavior. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20%40glaicer%2Fsupercode-sandbox-awareness%22) |
| @jeffreyjyz/opencode-command-code | 0.5.30 | ai | manifest准确描述了双provider、网络调用、plan gate和cmduse CLI渲染用法的功能；扫描到的fetch、atob、spawn均为与文档一致的正常行为，未发现注入、凭证外泄或混淆。 / the manifest accurately describes the two providers, network fetches, plan gating and cmduse CLI usage rendering; the flagged fetch/atob/spawn match documented behaviour with no injection, credential exfiltration or obfuscation. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20%40jeffreyjyz%2Fopencode-command-code%22) |
| Smartulf123/opencode-dictation | — | ai | 该插件为本地语音听写功能，核心逻辑（麦克风采集、Whisper转写、写入提示框）均本地运行，未见凭证外传、网络调用或注入等可疑行为 / The plugin implements local voice dictation (microphone capture, Whisper transcription, prompt injection) with no suspicious network calls, credential handling, or injection behavior detected | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20Smartulf123%2Fopencode-dictation%22) |
| opencode-git-plugin | 0.3.0 | ai | 插件仅通过本地shell调用git展示差异，明确声明不联系外部服务，描述与代码一致，未发现注入、凭据外泄或恶意网络调用。 / The plugin only runs git via a local shell API to display diffs, explicitly states it never contacts an external service, and the listing accurately matches the code, with no injection, exfiltration, or network calls found. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20opencode-git-plugin%22) |
| NikiHrs/opencode-free-model-watch | — | ai | 包仅做本地免费模型状态比较与提示，README明确声明不发送任何数据，未见网络调用、凭证外泄或注入等风险。 / The package only compares local free-model state and shows notices, with a README explicitly stating it sends no data anywhere, and shows no network calls, exfiltration, or injection risks. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20NikiHrs%2Fopencode-free-model-watch%22) |
